# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

+ BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
+ BorrowRecord::isOverdue
+ Library::addDocument
+ Library::registerMember
+ Member::borrowBook
+ Member::extendReturnDueDate
+ Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
+ Member::returnBook

04:09:27.893 [main] DEBUG org.springframework.web.client.DefaultRestClient -- Writing [ChatCompletionRequest[messages=[ChatCompletionMessage[rawContent=# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Member::returnBook
Summary: Attempts to return a borrowed book identified by its title. The member must currently hold the book (i.e., have a borrow record for that title), and the book must not be overdue (i.e., today <= returnDue). If both conditions are met, the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true. Otherwise, returns false.
Algorithm:
  for each record r in this.borrowRecords:
      if r.book.title equals bookTitle then
          if r.isOverdue(today) then
              return false
          else
              this.borrowRecords.remove(r)
              return true
          end if
      end if
  end for
  // No matching record found
  return false
Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), book.title (navigated from BorrowRecord -> Book -> Document)
Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the book to return; today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output: boolean — true if the return succeeds, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: bookTitle is not null; today is a valid date.
Post-condition: If the operation returns true, the matching borrow record is removed from this member's borrowRecords. If false, no change occurs. A return is rejected if the member does not hold the book or if the book is overdue.

Member::extendReturnDueDate
Summary: Attempts to extend the due date of a borrowed book identified by its title. The operation finds the borrow record for the given title in this member's records, then calls its extendDueDate(today) method. Returns the resulting due date.
Algorithm:
  for each record r in this.borrowRecords:
      if r.book.title equals bookTitle then
          return r.extendDueDate(today)
      end if
  end for
  // If no record found, return null (or throw exception, but requirement implies member holds the book)
  return null
Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), book.title (navigated from BorrowRecord -> Book -> Document)
Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the borrowed book; today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output: Date — the new due date after potential extension, or null if the member does not have a record for that title.
Pre-condition: bookTitle is not null; today is a valid date.
Post-condition: If a matching borrow record exists, its returnDue may be updated as per BorrowRecord::extendDueDate. The returned value equals the final returnDue of that record, or null if no match.

Member::borrowBook
Summary: Attempts to borrow a book by title for this member. The title must exist in the library's documents, the document must be a Book, the book must not be currently borrowed by any member, and this member must have fewer than 3 active borrow records. If all conditions are met, creates a BorrowRecord with today as borrowingDate and today+7 as returnDue, adds it to this member's borrowRecords, and returns true. Otherwise returns false.
Algorithm:
  if this member's borrowRecords size >= 3 then return false
  find document in library.documents where document.title equals bookTitle
  if document not found then return false
  if document is not of type Book then return false
  book = document as Book
  for each member m in library.members:
      for each record r in m.borrowRecords:
          if r.book equals book then return false
      end for
  end for
  create new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate=today, returnDue=today+7, book=book
  this.borrowRecords.add(newRecord)
  return true
Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), library (attribute of Member), library.documents, library.members (attributes of Library)
Related Classes: Library, Document, Book, BorrowRecord, Member
Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the book to borrow; today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output: boolean — true if borrowing succeeds, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date; bookTitle is not null.
Post-condition: If the operation returns true, a new BorrowRecord is added to this member's borrowRecords with the specified dates and book, and the book is considered borrowed. If false, no change occurs.

Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
Summary: Returns a list of titles of all books currently borrowed by this member.
Algorithm:
  titles = new empty List<String>
  for each record r in this.borrowRecords:
      titles.add(r.book.title)
  end for
  return titles
Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), book.title (navigated from BorrowRecord -> Book -> Document)
Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input: none
Output: List<String> — a list of book titles; may be empty if no borrow records exist.
Pre-condition: none
Post-condition: The returned list contains exactly the titles of all books in this member's borrowRecords, in no particular order.

```text
2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Member::returnBook
Summary: Attempts to return a borrowed book identified by its title. The member must currently hold the book (i.e., have a borrow record for that title), and the book must not be overdue (i.e., today <= returnDue). If both conditions are met, the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true. Otherwise, returns false.
Algorithm:
  for each record r in this.borrowRecords:
      if r.book.title equals bookTitle then
          if r.isOverdue(today) then
              return false
          else
              this.borrowRecords.remove(r)
              return true
          end if
      end if
  end for
  // No matching record found
  return false
Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), book.title (navigated from BorrowRecord -> Book -> Document)
Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the book to return; today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output: boolean — true if the return succeeds, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: bookTitle is not null; today is a valid date.
Post-condition: If the operation returns true, the matching borrow record is removed from this member's borrowRecords. If false, no change occurs. A return is rejected if the member does not hold the book or if the book is overdue.

Member::extendReturnDueDate
Summary: Attempts to extend the due date of a borrowed book identified by its title. The operation finds the borrow record for the given title in this member's records, then calls its extendDueDate(today) method. Returns the resulting due date.
Algorithm:
  for each record r in this.borrowRecords:
      if r.book.title equals bookTitle then
          return r.extendDueDate(today)
      end if
  end for
  // If no record found, return null (or throw exception, but requirement implies member holds the book)
  return null
Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), book.title (navigated from BorrowRecord -> Book -> Document)
Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the borrowed book; today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output: Date — the new due date after potential extension, or null if the member does not have a record for that title.
Pre-condition: bookTitle is not null; today is a valid date.
Post-condition: If a matching borrow record exists, its returnDue may be updated as per BorrowRecord::extendDueDate. The returned value equals the final returnDue of that record, or null if no match.

Member::borrowBook
Summary: Attempts to borrow a book by title for this member. The title must exist in the library's documents, the document must be a Book, the book must not be currently borrowed by any member, and this member must have fewer than 3 active borrow records. If all conditions are met, creates a BorrowRecord with today as borrowingDate and today+7 as returnDue, adds it to this member's borrowRecords, and returns true. Otherwise returns false.
Algorithm:
  if this member's borrowRecords size >= 3 then return false
  find document in library.documents where document.title equals bookTitle
  if document not found then return false
  if document is not of type Book then return false
  book = document as Book
  for each member m in library.members:
      for each record r in m.borrowRecords:
          if r.book equals book then return false
      end for
  end for
  create new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate=today, returnDue=today+7, book=book
  this.borrowRecords.add(newRecord)
  return true
Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), library (attribute of Member), library.documents, library.members (attributes of Library)
Related Classes: Library, Document, Book, BorrowRecord, Member
Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the book to borrow; today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output: boolean — true if borrowing succeeds, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date; bookTitle is not null.
Post-condition: If the operation returns true, a new BorrowRecord is added to this member's borrowRecords with the specified dates and book, and the book is considered borrowed. If false, no change occurs.

Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
Summary: Returns a list of titles of all books currently borrowed by this member.
Algorithm:
  titles = new empty List<String>
  for each record r in this.borrowRecords:
      titles.add(r.book.title)
  end for
  return titles
Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), book.title (navigated from BorrowRecord -> Book -> Document)
Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input: none
Output: List<String> — a list of book titles; may be empty if no borrow records exist.
Pre-condition: none
Post-condition: The returned list contains exactly the titles of all books in this member's borrowRecords, in no particular order.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Operation Specifications
Library::addDocument
Summary: Adds a Document object to the library's collection of documents.
Algorithm: documents.add(doc)
Related Features: documents (attribute of Library)
Related Classes: Document
Input: doc : Document — a document instance to add.
Output: void
Pre-condition: doc is not null.
Post-condition: documents contains doc.

Library::registerMember
Summary: Registers a new member with the given first name and surname. The name must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only, and must not be a duplicate of an existing member's full name (case-sensitive, trimmed comparison). On success, creates a Member object with no borrow records and adds it to the members list, returning true. On failure, returns false.
Algorithm:
  if firstName is null, empty, or whitespace-only then return false
  if surname is null, empty, or whitespace-only then return false
  trimmedFirstName = firstName.trim()
  trimmedSurname = surname.trim()
  for each member m in members:
      if m.firstName equals trimmedFirstName AND m.surname equals trimmedSurname then return false
  end for
  create new Member with firstName=trimmedFirstName, surname=trimmedSurname, borrowRecords=empty list, library=this
  members.add(newMember)
  return true
Related Features: name, members (attribute of Library)
Related Classes: Member
Input: firstName : String — the member's first name; surname : String — the member's surname.
Output: boolean — true if registration succeeds, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: none
Post-condition: If the operation returns true, a new Member is added to members with no borrow records, and the member's library reference points to this Library. If the operation returns false, members is unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary: Attempts to extend the return due date of this borrow record by seven days, but only if the given today's date is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged.
Algorithm:
  if (today is on or after borrowingDate AND today is strictly before returnDue) then
      set returnDue = returnDue + 7 days
  end if
  return returnDue
Related Features: borrowingDate, returnDue (attributes of BorrowRecord)
Related Classes: None
Input: today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output: Date — the (possibly updated) returnDue date.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date; borrowingDate and returnDue are valid dates with returnDue >= borrowingDate.
Post-condition: If the extension condition is met, returnDue is increased by exactly 7 days; otherwise, returnDue is unchanged. The returned value equals the final returnDue.

BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary: Checks whether this borrow record is overdue relative to the given today's date.
Algorithm: return (today > returnDue)
Related Features: returnDue (attribute of BorrowRecord)
Related Classes: None
Input: today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
Output: boolean — true if today is strictly after returnDue, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: today is a valid date; returnDue is a valid date.
Post-condition: The result is true if and only if today > returnDue.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;

  3.3 You should not change the method signature;

4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Attempts to borrow a book by title for this member.
   * Accepts only when the document with the given title exists, is a Book,
   * the book is not currently borrowed by any member, and this member holds fewer than three books.
   * A successful borrowing creates a BorrowRecord with today's date as borrowingDate
   * and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true.
   * Any rejected borrowing returns false.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if this member's borrowRecords size >= 3 then return false
   *   find document in library.documents where document.title equals bookTitle
   *   if document not found or document is not instance of Book then return false
   *   book = (Book) document
   *   for each member in library.members:
   *       for each record in member.borrowRecords:
   *           if record.book equals book then return false
   *   create new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate = today, returnDue = today + 7 days, book = book
   *   add record to this.borrowRecords
   *   return true
   * Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member), library (attribute of Member),
   *                   library.documents (attribute of Library), library.members (attribute of Library)
   * Related Classes: Book, BorrowRecord, Document, Library
   * Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the book to borrow.
   *        today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
   * Output: boolean — true if borrowing succeeds, false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: today is a valid date.
   * Post-condition: If true is returned, a new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate = today,
   *                 returnDue = today + 7 days, and book = the found Book has been added
   *                 to this member's borrowRecords.
   *                 If false is returned, borrowRecords is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    // Check if this member already holds 3 or more books
    if (getBorrowRecords().size() >= 3) {
      return false;
    }

    Library library = getLibrary();
    if (library == null) {
      return false;
    }

    // Find document in library.documents where document.title equals bookTitle
    Document foundDocument = null;
    for (Document document : library.getDocuments()) {
      if (document.getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        foundDocument = document;
        break;
      }
    }

    // If document not found or document is not instance of Book then return false
    if (foundDocument == null || !(foundDocument instanceof Book)) {
      return false;
    }

    Book book = (Book) foundDocument;

    // Check if the book is currently borrowed by any member
    for (Member member : library.getMembers()) {
      for (BorrowRecord record : member.getBorrowRecords()) {
        if (record.getBook().equals(book)) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }

    // Create new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate = today, returnDue = today + 7 days, book = book
    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    Date returnDue = new Date(today.getYear(), today.getMonth(), today.getDate() + 7);
    
    BorrowRecord borrowRecord = MunicipalLibraryFactory.eINSTANCE.createBorrowRecord();
    borrowRecord.setBorrowingDate(today);
    borrowRecord.setReturnDue(returnDue);
    borrowRecord.setBook(book);

    // Add record to this.borrowRecords
    getBorrowRecords().add(borrowRecord);

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Attempts to return a borrowed book by title for this member.
   * If the member does not currently hold that title, returns false.
   * If the borrowed book is overdue (today > returnDue), the return is rejected,
   * the borrow record remains active, and returns false.
   * Otherwise, the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.
   * Algorithm:
   *   find borrowRecord in this.borrowRecords where borrowRecord.book.title equals bookTitle
   *   if borrowRecord not found then return false
   *   if borrowRecord.isOverdue(today) then return false
   *   remove borrowRecord from this.borrowRecords
   *   return true
   * Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member)
   * Related Classes: BorrowRecord
   * Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the book to return.
   *        today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
   * Output: boolean — true if return succeeds, false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: today is a valid date.
   * Post-condition: If true is returned, the borrowRecord with the given bookTitle has been removed
   *                 from this member's borrowRecords.
   *                 If false is returned, borrowRecords is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean returnBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    // Find borrowRecord in this.borrowRecords where borrowRecord.book.title equals bookTitle
    BorrowRecord foundRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        foundRecord = record;
        break;
      }
    }

    // If borrowRecord not found then return false
    if (foundRecord == null) {
      return false;
    }

    // If borrowRecord.isOverdue(today) then return false
    if (foundRecord.isOverdue(today)) {
      return false;
    }

    // Remove borrowRecord from this.borrowRecords
    getBorrowRecords().remove(foundRecord);

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Attempts to extend the due date of a borrowed book by title.
   * When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date,
   * the due date is moved forward by seven days and the new due date is returned.
   * Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.
   * Algorithm:
   *   find borrowRecord in this.borrowRecords where borrowRecord.book.title equals bookTitle
   *   if borrowRecord not found then return null (or throw exception, but requirement implies member holds the book)
   *   return borrowRecord.extendDueDate(today)
   * Related Features: borrowRecords (attribute of Member)
   * Related Classes: BorrowRecord
   * Input: bookTitle : String — the title of the book whose due date is to be extended.
   *        today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
   * Output: Date — the new due date if extension succeeds, otherwise the original due date.
   * Pre-condition: today is a valid date; this member has a borrowRecord with the given bookTitle.
   * Post-condition: If today is on or after borrowingDate and strictly before returnDue,
   *                 then returnDue is increased by exactly 7 days.
   *                 Otherwise, returnDue is unchanged.
   *                 The returned value equals the final returnDue.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model dataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date" todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public Date extendReturnDueDate(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    // Find borrowRecord in this.borrowRecords where borrowRecord.book.title equals bookTitle
    BorrowRecord foundRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        foundRecord = record;
        break;
      }
    }

    // If borrowRecord not found then return null
    if (foundRecord == null) {
      return null;
    }

    // Delegate to borrowRecord.extendDueDate(today)
    return foundRecord.extendDueDate(today);
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Returns a list of titles of all books currently borrowed by this member.

   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<String> listBorrowedBookTitles()
  {
    EList<String> titles = new BasicEList<String>();
    for (BorrowRecord borrowRecord : getBorrowRecords()) {
      titles.add(borrowRecord.getBook().getTitle());
    }
    return titles;
  }
```

   *                 to this member's borrowRecords.
   *                 If false is returned, borrowRecords is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    if (getBorrowRecords().size() >= 3) {
      return false;
    }
    Library library = getLibrary();
    if (library == null) {
      return false;
    }
    Document foundDocument = null;
    for (Document document : library.getDocuments()) {
      if (document.getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        foundDocument = document;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (foundDocument == null || !(foundDocument instanceof Book)) {
      return false;
    }
    Book book = (Book) foundDocument;
    for (Member member : library.getMembers()) {
      for (BorrowRecord record : member.getBorrowRecords()) {
        if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().equals(book)) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    Date returnDue = new Date(today.getYear(), today.getMonth(), today.getDate() + 7);
    BorrowRecord borrowRecord = MunicipalLibraryFactory.eINSTANCE.createBorrowRecord();
    borrowRecord.setBorrowingDate(today);
    borrowRecord.setReturnDue(returnDue);
    borrowRecord.setBook(book);
    getBorrowRecords().add(borrowRecord);
    return true;
  }

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Attempts to return a borrowed book by title for this member.
   * If the member does not currently hold that title, returns false.
   * If the borrowed book is overdue (today > returnDue), the return is rejected,
   * the borrow record remains active, and returns false.
   * Otherwise, the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.
   * Algorithm:
   *   find borrowRecord in this.borrowRecords where borrowRecord.book.title equals bookTitle
   *   if borrowRecord not found then return false
   *   if borrowRecord.isOverdue(today) then return false
   *   remove borrowRecord from this.borrowRecords

   * Output: boolean — true if return succeeds, false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: today is a valid date.
   * Post-condition: If true is returned, the borrowRecord with the given bookTitle has been removed
   *                 from this member's borrowRecords.
   *                 If false is returned, borrowRecords is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT

  public boolean returnBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    BorrowRecord foundRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        foundRecord = record;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (foundRecord == null) {
      return false;
    }
    if (foundRecord.isOverdue(today)) {
      return false;
    }
    getBorrowRecords().remove(foundRecord);
    return true;
  }

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * @model dataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date" todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public Date extendDueDate(final Date today)
  {
    if (!today.before(getBorrowingDate()) && today.before(getReturnDue()))
    {
      long newTime = getReturnDue().getTime() + 7L * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
      setReturnDue(new Date(newTime));
    }
    return getReturnDue();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Checks whether this borrow record is overdue relative to the given today's date.
   * Algorithm: return (today > returnDue)
   * Related Features: returnDue (attribute of BorrowRecord)
   * Related Classes: None
   * Input: today : Date — the current date in "yyyy-MM-dd" format.
   * Output: boolean — true if today is strictly after returnDue, false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: today is a valid date; returnDue is a valid date.
   * Post-condition: The result is true if and only if today > returnDue.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean isOverdue(final Date today)
  {
    return today.after(getReturnDue());
  }
